Canary-first: exercise the ladder (goal -> pot -> plan -> work-item -> cup) on a disposable objective BEFORE the real mobile-apps goal.\n\nTEMPLATE DECISIONS (pre-answered; the papercusp-webapp template mandates both at materialization):\n- tenancy = SINGLE-USER LOCAL install (embedded/local PG, papercusp-style) — the cheaper fork.\n- domain = READING LIST: url, title, tags, status, addedAt. A single-noun ledger that exercises schema -> panel -> grid -> lexicon with zero external deps, zero secrets, and no owner-walled tail (no app-store, no domain registration, no payments) — which matters because the GOAL contract's clause-5 walls would otherwise stall the dry run on an owner gate.\n\nCOMPOSITION ORDER (papercusp-webapp is a THIN composition, components: []): papercusp-web-host -> papercusp-data-layer -> papercusp-ui. The composed app must pass the UNION of boot-e2e + components-integrated + composition-integrity.\n\nSCOPE LIMIT (disclosed assumption): under capacity factor 0.25 / maxCups 1 a full build on this chassis is NOT a cheap single-cup job. Acceptance here is 'the ladder carries work' — a cup driving a work-item to a terminal state whose owner is the CUP and not the placer, read via tool_invocations.role='cup' per D-014 (NOT adv_sessions, which an orchestrator-placed headless cup never writes — WI-36281). This plan deliberately does NOT attempt the full app.","activeOwners":[]},{"harness":"panda-gallery","slug":"panda-gallery-v1-2026-08-10","title":"Panda gallery v1 — a website displaying panda images over an animated background","status":"awaiting-acceptance","created":"2026-08-10","archived":false,"template":null,"origin":"local","basis":"unverified","ownerRefs":[],"creationRef":"plan_revision:panda-gallery:panda-gallery-v1-2026-08-10:1","creationEvidence":"Delivers goal make-a-website-displaying-images-of-pandas-aa0205. diagnosis for EI-21869958222942655 (plans.list crossed its documented cardinality windowing trigger — sync-read-audit.ts flags it at 1540 rows / ~1176 KiB, over the 720KB allowlisted ceiling). Measured live: row growth is 100% legitimate active-plan authoring (1424/1523 rows in the papercusp harness are archived=false AND not scheduled-instances — no housekeeping/pruning shortcut analogous to the coord.plans snapshot-instance fix is available). A naive row-limit on the shared usePlanList() cache would silently truncate the Shipped/Superseded bucket tabs in BOTH full-browse UIs (PlanRail/admin plans page, and PlansListPanel/the Create-tab) — a real product regression, not an edge case. This plan captures the full consumer audit and the concrete lazy-per-bucket-fetch design that preserves complete reachability.
